              	; stack+8	Smooth Scroll 
              	; 2a6ch		Scroll Window Len (blokiranje dela video mem)
              	; stack+1	Link
              	
0000:         		org	0
              	
0000: F3      	        DI
0001: 97      	        SUB     A
0002: C3DA03  	        JP      Z18E
0005: DF      	ZAREZ   RST     Z8      ; IF (DE)<>","  GO "WHAT"
0006: 2C      	        DEFB    ','     ; ELSE RST Z807
0007: 2C      	        DEFB    GO8C9-$-1  ; this is WHAT? ept
              	; 0x08 =================
0008: CDB20A  	Z807    CALL    Z24     ; PRETVORI SLEDECI ASCII NIZ U INTEGER I SMESTI GA U HL
000B: C36D0A  	        JP      ZC0B
000E: F3      	FAST    DI              ; FAST FLAG
000F: C9      	        RET
              	; 0x10 =================
0010: 7C      	Z32     LD      A,H     ; CP HL-DE Z & C FLAGS VALID
0011: BA      	        CP      D
0012: C0      	        RET     NZ
0013: 7D      	        LD      A,L
0014: BB      	        CP      E
0015: C9      	        RET
0016: FB      	SLOW    EI              ; SLOW FLAG
0017: C9      	        RET
              	; 0x18 =================
              	
              	; if (DE) = ($+1) then skip ($+2) and  PC <---- $+3
              	; else  PC <---- PC+$+2
              	
              	; In pseudo-code:
              	;
              	; If (**SP) == (EAT_SPACE(DE)) Then
              	;	*SP=*SP+2
              	; 	DE=DE+1
              	; Else
              	;	*SP=*SP+2+(*(*SP+1))		
              	; End
              	
              	; Parameters:
              	;	DE: Pointer to a string.
              	; Returns:
              	;	DE: Skips leading space and increments DE, if there is a match.
              	;	A: First non-space character in DE.
              	;	Zf: Set if character matches, reset if not.
              	; Destroys: 
              	;	flags.
              	
              	
0018: E3      	Z8      EX      (SP),HL ; IF ((SP))=(DE) THEN (SP)=(SP)+2
              	                        ; ELSE (SP)=((SP)+1)+(SP)+2
0019: CD0501  	        CALL    Z40     ; skip blanks, pointer de
001C: BE      	        CP      (HL)
001D: C39401  	        JP      Z98
              	
              	; 0x20 =================
0020: D9      	Z16     EXX             ; PRINT CHAR IN A ON SCREEN
0021: FE20    	        CP      20H
0023: CDB509  	        CALL    ZACB
0026: D9      	        EXX
0027: C9      	        RET
              	; 0x28 =================
0028: 210000  	HL0     LD      HL,0
002B: C9      	        RET
002C: CCCC    	ZEB9    DEFW    0CCCCH  ; RND CONST
002E: CC7E    	        DEFW    7ECCH
              	; 0x30 =================
0030: F1      	Z48     POP     AF      ; GO NEXT INSTRUCTION
0031: CD1404  	        CALL    Z8B3
0034: C38F07  	GO8C9   JP      WHAT
0037: 2A      	NUMBER  DEFB    42      ;       VERZIJA 42
              	
              		org	0x38
              	
              	; -------------------- INTERRUPT -----------------------
              	; B = Value with bit 7 set/res for R reg adjustment
              	; C = current line number 80h - 8Fh
              	; D = char gen Row addr 0 - 11
              	; E = scan line counter 12 - 1 
              	; HL = latadr
              	
0038: F5      	        PUSH    AF	; \   I N T   V I D E O
0039: C5      	        PUSH    BC	; \\
003A: D5      	        PUSH    DE	; \\\
003B: E5      	        PUSH    HL	; \\\\
              	
              	; -------------------- SMOOTH SCROLL --------------------- 
              	
003C: 21B02B  	        LD      HL,stack+8   ; SCROLL COUNT (ini 3, scroll je u 3 faze)
003F: 3EB0    	        LD      A,0b0H
0041: 96      	        SUB     (HL)
0042: 96      	        SUB     (HL)
0043: 96      	        SUB     (HL)		; Subtract 3 * SCROLL_CNT from A
              					; (jer preskace po 3 linije)
0044: 5F      	        LD      E,A		; smooth scroll counter to E
0045: 7E      	        LD      A,(HL)
0046: 0F      	        RRCA
0047: 0F      	        RRCA
0048: 0F      	        RRCA                    ; same as 5x SLA, which is x32
0049: 47      	        LD      B,A		; for DJNZ loop
004A: B7      	        OR      A
004B: 2800    	        JR      Z,$+2
004D: 2805    	        JR      Z,Scr
004F: 35      	        DEC     (HL)            ; decremenf offset to animate smooth scroll
0050: AF      	        XOR     A
0051: D8      	SPECL   RET     C               ; C is never set, used for timing only!
0052: 10FD    	        DJNZ    SPECL
0054: 23      	Scr     INC     HL		; Load SCROLL_FLAG address to HL
0055: 77      	        LD      (HL),A  	; set/reset scroll flag (scroll done, if any)
0056: 43      	        LD      B,E		; multiscroll cycle counter to B
              	
0057: C35BA8  	        jp      hor_loop        ; go to hor pos service

              	 ; read from table

007F: 04      	        INC     B		; 4    B = current scan line 00h...0Fh

0084: 1600    	        ld      D,0             ; 7   d <--- 0
0086: 58      	        ld      E,B             ; 4   de = table offset
0087: 19      	        add     hl,de           ; 11  now HL points to <DeltaR,I,R> in lookup table
0088: D8      		ret	C		; 5   TIMING ONLY!!!
              	
0089: 46      	        ld      B,(hl)          ; 7   ...get delta R (step)
008A: 23      	        inc     HL              ; 6
008B: 7E      	        ld      a,(hl)          ; 7   ...get I (page)
008C: ED47    	        ld      i,a             ; 9
008E: 23      	        inc     HL              ; 6

0090: 213F20  	        ld      hl,latadr       ; 10  latch addr

              	 ; send first 16 blocks unconditionally

              	 ; send all used blocks (16 - 1023)

              	 ; CHS

AD1B: 13      		inc	DE
AD1C: 2B      		dec	HL
AD1D: 7C      		ld	A,H
AD1E: B5      		or	L
AD1F: 20F4    		jr	nz,sbloop	; 512 x
              	
AD21: E1      		pop	HL	  ; //
AD22: D1      		pop	DE	  ; /
AD23: C9      		ret
              	
              	; ************************************************************************
2C2E:         	blk_tot		equ BUF+120	; Blocks total
2C30:         	blk_cou		equ BUF+122	; Block count
              	; ************************************************************************
AD24:         	RESTORE
AD24: 1192BA  	        LD      DE,message_f_yn	; "DELETE ALL STORED DATA? (Y/N)?"
AD27: CD3709  	        CALL    Z94F		; print it on screen
AD2A: CD9CA9  		call	ny		; wait for N/ESC or Y key, go to STOP_RELEASE if N/ESC
              	 ; restore unconditionally
AD2D: 3E0D    		ld	a,0x0d
AD2F: 32B62B  		ld	(BUF),a		; to avoid repeating of "RESTORE" command
AD32: F3      		DI
AD33: AF      		xor	a
AD34: 32FE9D  		ld	(chs),a		; ini CHS adder
              	; ------- Galaksija sends to PC
AD37: F3      		DI			; DI
AD38: 3E65    		ld	a,0x65
AD3A: CD7AB7  	        call    txbyte		; command byte 0x65  1 ---------->
AD3D: 3E97    		ld	a,0x97
AD3F: CD7AB7  	        call    txbyte		; command byte 0x97  1 ---------->
              	
              	 ;;; receive 1st raport -------------------------------------------------------
              	
AD42: CDD0B7  		call	rxbyte	 	;      <---------- 1
AD45: FEAA    		cp	0xAA		; poruka greske?
AD47: C21BB9  		jp	nz,ERR11	; unrecognized reply from PC
AD4A: CDD0B7  		call	rxbyte	 	;      <---------- 1
AD4D: FE39    		cp	0x39
AD4F: C21BB9  		jp	nz,ERR11	; unrecognized reply from PC
              	 ; receive parser
AD52: CDD0B7  		call	rxbyte   	;      <---------- 1
AD55: FEF0    		cp	0xF0
AD57: 2812    		jr	z,cont_restore
AD59: FEF2    		cp	0xF2
AD5B: CA25B9  		jp	z,ERR13		; FILE NOT FOUND IN PC FOLDER
AD5E: FEF5    		cp	0xF5
AD60: CA2FB9  		jp	z,ERR16		; BAD FILE FORMAT
AD63: FEF6    		cp	0xF6
AD65: CAF3B8  		jp	z,ERR3		; Error on serial line
AD68: C31BB9  		jp	ERR11		; UNRECOGNIZED REPLY FROM PC
AD6B:         	cont_restore
              	; ------- PC sends to Galaksija
AD6B: AF      		xor	a
AD6C: 32FE9D  		ld	(chs),a		; ini CHS adder
AD6F: 010000  		ld	BC,0
AD72: ED43302C		ld	(blk_cou),BC	; block counter
AD76: CDD0B7  		call	rxbyte		;       <---------- 1
AD79: FECC    		cp	0xCC		; CC
AD7B: C28F07  		jp	nz,WHAT		; nije stiglo CC
AD7E: CDD3B7  		call	rxbyte2		;       <---------- 1
AD81: FE36    		cp	0x36		; 36
AD83: C28F07  		jp	nz,WHAT		; nije stiglo 36	
              	 ; synced CC 36, get # of blocks
AD86: CDD3B7  		call	rxbyte2		;       <---------- 1
AD89: 322E2C  		ld	(blk_tot),a	; blocks total
AD8C: CDD3B7  		call	rxbyte2		;       <---------- 1
AD8F: 322F2C  		ld	(blk_tot+1),a	; blocks total
AD92:         	go_blocks			; <====================================
AD92: CDD0B7  		call	rxbyte		;       <---------- 1
AD95: 4F      		ld	C,A		; block addr lo
AD96: CDD3B7  		call	rxbyte2		;       <---------- 1
AD99: 47      		ld	B,A		; block addr hi
AD9A: C5      		push	BC	  ; \
AD9B: 11009E  		ld	DE,FlBuf
AD9E: 0600    		ld	B,0
ADA0:         	get512by			; <==================
ADA0: CDD3B7  		call	rxbyte2		;       <---------- 1 (256)
ADA3: 12      		ld	(DE),A
ADA4: 13      		inc	DE
ADA5: CDD3B7  		call	rxbyte2		;       <---------- 1 (256)
ADA8: 12      		ld	(DE),A
ADA9: 13      		inc	DE
ADAA: 10F4    		djnz	get512by	; ==================>

              	; push de

              	; call	PrintHL

              	; pop	de
              	; pop bc

ADBF: 2802    		jr	z,done_restore
ADC1: 30CF    		jr	nc,go_blocks	; ====================================>
ADC3:         	done_restore

ADCA: BB      		cp	E
ADCB: C2F8B8  		jp	nz,ERR4		; bad checksum
ADCE: C36600  		jp	0x0066		; OK

              			; A=len(to-from), at exit: Z = match, NZ = no match

AFBF: 3600    		ld	(hl),0		; erase it too
AFC1: 23      		inc	hl
AFC2: 7E      		ld	a,(hl)
AFC3: 3600    		ld	(hl),0		; erase it too
AFC5: E603    		and	3		; 10-bit range
AFC7: 47      		ld	b,a		; bc = block #
AFC8: C5      		push	bc	; \
              	 ; write back block 1...15
AFC9: ED4B2D2C		ld	bc,(memorized+1); C = block # (1...15)
AFCD: 0600    		ld	b,0		; BC = Block # (1...15)
AFCF: CDFBAA  		call	write_block_fb	; @@@@ NAME write back block with name
              	 ; read block 0 with occupancy bits
AFD2: 010000  		ld	bc,0
AFD5: CD95AB  		call	read_block_fb	; **** BITS read block 0 with occupancy bits
AFD8: C1      		pop	bc	; /
AFD9:         	go_clear			; <-------------------------------
AFD9: C5      		push	bc	; \	; bc = block #
AFDA: CDECAF  		call	clear_block_bit	; clear block bit BC in FlBuf
AFDD: C1      		pop	bc	; /
AFDE: CD2FAB  		call	read_last_two	; rd last two bytes of block BC to reg BC
AFE1: 78      		ld	a,b
AFE2: B1      		or	c
AFE3: 20F4    		jr	nz,go_clear	; ------------------------------->
              	 ; write back block 0
AFE5: 010000  		ld	bc,0
AFE8: CDFBAA  		call	write_block_fb	; @@@@ BITS wr modif block 0 back to flash mem
AFEB: C9      		ret
              	
              	; -------------------------------
AFEC:         	clear_block_bit		; clear block bit BC in FlBuf (0x0010...0x03FF)
AFEC: 1600    		ld	d,0
              	    rept 3          ; --------	; /8
              		srl	b
              		rr	c		; BC = pozicija bajta u matrici blokova (0x10-0x3FF)
              		rr	d		; d = broj bita u bajtu <5 (0x20...0xE0 step 8)
              	    endm	    ; --------
AFEE: CB38    		srl	b
AFF0: CB19    		rr	c		; BC = pozicija bajta u matrici blokova (0x10-0x3FF)
AFF2: CB1A    		rr	d		; d = broj bita u bajtu <5 (0x20...0xE0 step 8)
AFF4: CB38    		srl	b
AFF6: CB19    		rr	c		; BC = pozicija bajta u matrici blokova (0x10-0x3FF)
AFF8: CB1A    		rr	d		; d = broj bita u bajtu <5 (0x20...0xE0 step 8)
AFFA: CB38    		srl	b
AFFC: CB19    		rr	c		; BC = pozicija bajta u matrici blokova (0x10-0x3FF)
AFFE: CB1A    		rr	d		; d = broj bita u bajtu <5 (0x20...0xE0 step 8)
B000: 21FE9D  		ld	hl,FlBuf-2	; -2 jer treba da krene od bloka -0x10
B003: 09      		add	hl,bc		; addr of block bit to be erased
B004: 7A      		ld	a,d		; a = 0x20...0xE0 (XXX00000)
B005: 07      		rlca
B006: 07      		rlca
B007: 07      		rlca			; a = 0..7 (broj bita koji treba resetovati)
              	; reset bit 0...7 in (HL) if a=0...7
B008: B7      		or	a
B009: 2002    		jr	nz,$+4
B00B: CB86    		res	0,(HL)
B00D: 3D      		dec	a
B00E: 2002    		jr	nz,$+4
B010: CB8E    		res	1,(hl)
B012: 3D      		dec	a
B013: 2002    		jr	nz,$+4
B015: CB96    		res	2,(hl)
B017: 3D      		dec	a
B018: 2002    		jr	nz,$+4
B01A: CB9E    		res	3,(hl)
B01C: 3D      		dec	a
B01D: 2002    		jr	nz,$+4
B01F: CBA6    		res	4,(hl)
B021: 3D      		dec	a
B022: 2002    		jr	nz,$+4
B024: CBAE    		res	5,(hl)
B026: 3D      		dec	a
B027: 2002    		jr	nz,$+4
B029: CBB6    		res	6,(hl)
B02B: 3D      		dec	a
B02C: 2002    		jr	nz,$+4
B02E: CBBE    		res	7,(hl)
B030: C9      		ret
              	
              	; -----------------------------
B031:         	setbit			; set bit 0...7 in (DE) if a=0...7
B031: EB      		ex	de,hl	   ; X
B032: B7      		or	a
B033: 2002    		jr	nz,$+4		; 0
B035: CBC6    		set	0,(HL)
B037: 3D      		dec	a
B038: 2002    		jr	nz,$+4		; 1
B03A: CBCE    		set	1,(HL)
B03C: 3D      		dec	a
B03D: 2002    		jr	nz,$+4		; 2
B03F: CBD6    		set	2,(HL)
B041: 3D      		dec	a
B042: 2002    		jr	nz,$+4		; 3
B044: CBDE    		set	3,(HL)
B046: 3D      		dec	a
B047: 2002    		jr	nz,$+4		; 4
B049: CBE6    		set	4,(HL)
B04B: 3D      		dec	a
B04C: 2002    		jr	nz,$+4		; 5
B04E: CBEE    		set	5,(HL)
B050: 3D      		dec	a
B051: 2002    		jr	nz,$+4		; 6
B053: CBF6    		set	6,(HL)
B055: 3D      		dec	a
B056: 2002    		jr	nz,$+4		; 7
B058: CBFE    		set	7,(HL)
B05A: EB      		ex	de,hl	   ; X
B05B: C9      		ret
              	
              	; ------------------------------------------
B05C:         	locate_filename   ; find filename (DE), Z:found, H=block#, L=name#, BC=len of pgm, DE=after fn
              			  ; NZ:not found, H=block#, L=free name#, DE = start of filename in mem
B05C: CD22B1  		call	skip_quote	; skip blanks and quote, if any
B05F: ED532A2C		ld	(start_of_fn),de ; = start of filename
B063: CD2DB1  		call	count_chars	; count (de) valid chars (out: a = filename length)
B066: 32302C  		ld	(fn_len),a	; filename length
B069: 3D      		dec	a
B06A: FE0E    		cp	14
B06C: D211B9  		jp	nc,ERR9		; if filename lenght <1 or >14 chars = irregular name length
              	 ; start testing up to 15 blocks, (BUF+0) = filename ptr, H = filename length
B06F: 010100  		ld	bc,1		; start with block 1 (ignore block 0)
B072: ED432E2C		ld	(current),bc	; bc = current block #
B076:         	go_find_bl		  ; find block with exact name	<================= 1
B076: CD95AB  	        call    read_block_fb	; rd 512 by block BC ---> Flash Buffer
B079: 21009E  		ld	hl,FlBuf	; flash buf start addr (L #87654 = filename count)
B07C:         	go_find_name		; find name  0...31	<============ 2
              	 ; start compare names inside 1 block
B07C: 3A302C  		ld	a,(fn_len)	; filename length
B07F: 47      		ld	b,a		; B = filename len
B080: ED5B2A2C		ld	de,(start_of_fn) ; DE points to start of filename in memory
              	
B084: 7E      		ld	a,(hl)		; --1st-- char in the name (should be 0 if not used)
B085: CD4CB1  		call	isitchar
B088: 3005    		jr	nc,no_memo	; it is a valid char, do not memorize	----->
B08A: CD39B1  		call	memorize	; this position is unused, memorize it
B08D: 1813    		jr	fail1		; free space, no file here	-------------->
B08F:         	no_memo
              	
B08F:         	go_test_char		  ; cp chars loop start 1...(B)	<======== 3
B08F: 1A      		ld	a,(de)
B090: 13      		inc	de
B091: BE      		cp	(hl)
B092: 23      		inc	hl
B093: 200D    		jr	nz,fail1	; no match		-------------->
B095: 10F8    		djnz	go_test_char	; cp chars loop end	========> 3
              	; check if next (hl)=0 or L (bits 3210) = 14, otherwise names do not match
B097: AF      		xor	a
B098: BE      		cp	(hl)
B099: 283F    		jr	z,fn_match	; all bytes equal after B chars  ==========> FOUND
B09B: 7D      		ld	a,L
B09C: E60F    		and	0x0F
B09E: FE0E    		cp	14
B0A0: 2838    		jr	z,fn_match	; all bytes equal after B chars  ==========> FOUND
              	; continues to fail1...
              	; -------------------------------
B0A2:         	fail1
B0A2: 7D      		ld	a,l
B0A3: F60F    		or	0x0F
B0A5: 6F      		ld	l,a
B0A6: 23      		inc	hl		; HL points to the next name
B0A7: 3EA0    		ld	a,hi(FlBuf+512)	; end of FlBuf+1
B0A9: BC      		cp	h
B0AA: C27CB0  		jp	nz,go_find_name	; name loop end		============> 2
              	; end of buffer, match not found
B0AD: ED4B2E2C		ld	bc,(current)	; bc = current block #
B0B1: 03      		inc	bc		; next
B0B2: ED432E2C		ld	(current),bc	; bc = current block #
B0B6: CB61    		bit	4,c
B0B8: CA76B0  		jp	z,go_find_bl	; find block end	=================> 1
              	; name not found in 15 blocks, use (memorized)
B0BB: 2A2C2C  		ld	hl,(memorized)	; H=block # with free name, L=free name #
B0BE: ED5B2A2C		ld	de,(start_of_fn) ; DE = start of filename in mem
              	 ; calculate de after filename and quote (if any)
B0C2: 3A302C  		ld	a,(fn_len)	; filename length (1...14)
B0C5: 6F      		ld	l,a

B0D7: F6FF    		or	0xFF		; NZ:not found, H=block#, L=free name#
B0D9: C9      		ret

B0DA:         	fn_match	; filename match found! Get pgm length         <=========== FOUND

B0F8: 23      		inc	hl
B0F9: 46      		ld	b,(hl)		; bc = from
B0FA: 23      		inc	hl
B0FB: 7E      		ld	a,(hl)
B0FC: 23      		inc	hl
B0FD: 66      		ld	h,(hl)

B0FF: B7      		or	a		; Clear Carry
B100: ED42    		sbc	hl,bc		; HL = file len in by (from flash storage)
B102: 44      		ld	b,h
B103: 4D      		ld	c,l		; BC = file len in by (from flash storage)
B104: ED5B2A2C		ld	de,(start_of_fn) ; DE points to start of filename in memory
              	 ; calculate de after filename and quote (if any)
              					; skip fn loop start	<======== 3

B113: 2A2C2C  		ld	hl,(memorized)	; H=block (1-15), L=pos inside block (0-31)
B116: AF      		xor	a		; set Z flag
B117: C9      		ret
              	
              	; ----------------------------------------------------------------
B118:         	cp_de_hl		; compare (de):(hl) filename, length B

              	; ------------------------------------------------------

B160: FB      		ei

B1CB:         	get_blocks	    ; at exit: HL = number of free blocks (0...1024) (~135 ms)
B1CB: 010000  		ld	bc,0		; block 0

              						; hl = # of free blocks in flash (0...1024)

              	      rept 8          ; --- 8
              		rrca			; bit 0 first
              		jr	c,$+3		; if block used

B1E5: 0F      		rrca			; bit 0 first

B1F5: 0F      		rrca			; bit 0 first

B1F9: 0F      		rrca			; bit 0 first

              	; ------------------------------------------------------

B210:         	bytes_to_blocks		; input HL bytes, output DE blocks (HL must be >0)

B2EA: 329B2A  		ld	(BlokReg),a

              	 ; done
              	 ; read block 0
B2EF: 010000  		ld	bc,0

              	 ; write back block 0

2C10:         	FLAG_X		equ BUF+90	; bit 7: load_from_pc, other bits: ascii +|-|=

              					; hi = # of byte (0-125)

              	 ; decided: save program

              	 ; file saving starts now, find the 1st free block (block 0 already in FlBuf)

              	 ; filename
B39D: ED4B2D2C		ld	bc,(memorized+1) ; hi = # of block (1-15)
B3A1: 0600    		ld	b,0		; bc = # of block (1-15)

B3AD: 29      		add	hl,hl		; x8

              	 ; fill gap with zeros

              	;	call	show1	; ************************************

              	; ----------------------------------------------------------
B421:         	can_it_fit		; test da li program moze da stane u flash (go HOW if not)

B43E: DA0CB9  		jp	c,ERR8		; if not enough place in flash!
B441: C9      		ret
              	
              	; ----------------------------------------------------------
B442:         	find_0_in_FlBuf	; find unused block in the FlBuf, update free_bits and next_block
B442: 3EFF    		ld	a,0xFF
B444: 21009E  		ld	hl,FlBuf
B447: 067E    		ld	b,128-2
B449:         	go126

B44D: 10FA    		djnz	go126
B44F: C35301  		jp	SORRY		; not found byte <255 (should never come here)
B452:         	found_free_space
B452: 54      		ld	d,h
B453: 5D      		ld	e,l		; DE <---- HL (physical addr of byte with bit 0)
B454: 7E      		ld	a,(HL)		; a = byte with bit 0 (occupancy bits)
B455: 65      		ld	H,L		; H : # of byte (0...125)
B456: 6F      		ld	L,a		; L : bit arrangement (00000000-11111111)
B457: 22222C  	 	ld	(free_bits),HL	; low : bit arrangement, high: # of byte
              	; HL <---- H
B45A: 6C      		ld	l,h
B45B: 2600    		ld	h,0		; HL = # of byte in block bit area (0...125)
              	; HL <---- hl * 8
B45D: 29      		add	hl,hl		; # of byte x2
B45E: 29      		add	hl,hl		; # of byte x4
B45F: 29      		add	hl,hl		; # of byte x8
              	; HL <---- HL+0x10
B460: 011000  		ld	bc,0x10
B463: 09      		add	hl,bc
              	; count one's in A from right and add to HL, copy to BC
B464: CD6FB4  		call	get_zero	; in: A = occupancy bits, out: C = # of 1's from right
B467: 79      		ld	a,c

B46A: 09      		add	hl,bc

B46E: C9      		ret			; NOTE: keep DE and A at exit, SETBIT needs them!
              	
              	; ----------------------------------------------------------
B46F:         	get_zero		; in: A = occupancy bits, out: C = number of 1's from right
B46F: 0EFF    		ld	c,0xFF
B471: B9      		cp	c

B742: 12      		ld	(DE),a		; filename 14 ASCII bytes
B743: 13      		inc	DE
B744: 10F9    		djnz	go_rx_14

B752: CDD3B7  		call	rxbyte2		;       <----------

              	;;; receive file

              	; *****************************************************************
              	;       S E R I A L     T X        ********************************

              	; 1T = 0.3333 uS
              	; 1 bit @ 19200 baud = 52.08 uS = 156 T/bit
B77A:         	txbyte			; Tx A reg (19200 baud = 156 T/bit)  ABC
B77A: 4F      		ld	c,a

B77F: 32FE9D  		ld	(chs),a

B788: 0608    		ld	b,8		; 7   bit counter
B78A:         	tx8
B78A: CB09    		rrc	c		; 8
B78C: CD92B7  		call	txbit		; 17 + 118 = 135 each (data bits)
B78F: 10F9    		djnz	tx8		; 13|8
B791: 37      		scf			; 4   stop bit
B792:         	txbit				;     sub 118T + 17T call
B792: 3E00    		ld	a,0b00000000	; 7   for timing only
B794: 3E00    		ld	a,0b00000000	; 7
B796: 1F      		rra			; 4   Carry to bit 7,A
B797: 323F20  		ld	(latadr),a	; 13
B79A: 3E05    		ld	a,5		; 7
B79C:         	txbaud
B79C: 3D      		dec	a		; 4 x 5 = 20
B79D: C29CB7  		jp	nz,txbaud	; 10 x 5 = 50
B7A0: C9      		ret			; 10

              	; *****************************************************************
              	;       S E R I A L     R X        ********************************
              	; *****************************************************************
              	; *****************************************************************
              	
B7A1: C30503  	STOP2   jp      STOP
              	;________________________________
              	
              	; 1T = 0.3333 uS
              	; 1 bit @ 19200 baud = 52.08 uS = 156 T/bit
              	; on exit: Carry set = reception OK, byte in A and C
              	;          NC, NZ    = framing error (no stop bit), go to WHAT?
              	;          NC, Z     = reception interrupted (BRK), go to BREAK
              	
B7A4:         	rxbyte_spec        ; (1st byte only!) Rx A reg (19200 baud = 156 T/bit) (234 T/1.5 bit)
B7A4: 213A20  	        ld      hl,0x203A       ; 10    Rx bit addr
B7A7:         	rxloop_1                   ; \
B7A7: 3A3120  	        ld      a,(0x2031)      ; 13
B7AA: 0F      	        rrca                    ; 4
B7AB: 30F4    	        jr      nc,STOP2        ; 7     if BREAK hit
B7AD: 7E      	        ld	a,(hl)		; 7
B7AE: 0F      	        rrca                    ; 4
B7AF: DAA7B7  	        jp      c,rxloop_1 ; /  ; 10    loop 45 T, wait for LOW input
              	                        ;   ************ here ~37 T (+/- 23 T) after start bit \_ edge
              	 ;  time optimization
B7B2: 3A102C  		ld	a,(FLAG_X)	;;;  +|-|=  13 t
B7B5: FEBD    		cp	'='+0x80	;;;  +|-|=   7 t (PC flag is set, that's why +0x80)
B7B7: 2009    		jr	nz,not_exact	;;;  +|-|=   7/12 t (32 t after jr, 27 t if no jr)
              	 
              	; exact			; comes with 13+7+7=27 t
B7B9: ED5B202C		ld	de,(displacement) ;;;  =    20 t
B7BD: 3E07    		ld	a,7		; ;;;  =     7 t  ******************************
B7BF: C3E1B7  		jp	skr_rx		; ;;;  =    10 t   (64 t)
              	                        ;   ************ here ~101 t after start bit \_ edge
              	
B7C2:         	not_exact			; comes with 13+7+12=32 t
B7C2: 00      		nop			;;;    4 t
B7C3: 2A202C  		ld	hl,(displacement) ;;; 16 t
B7C6: 19      		add	hl,de		;;;   11 t  hl = (displacement) + (from)
B7C7: EB      		ex	de,hl		;;;    4 t  de = (displacement) + (from)
B7C8: 213A20  		ld	hl,0x203A	;;;   10 t  Rx bit addr
B7CB: 3E05    		ld	a,5		;;;    7 t        ******************************
B7CD: C3E1B7  		jp	skr_rx		;;;   10 t       (90 t)
              	                        ;   ************ here ~131 t after start bit \_ edge

B7D0: 213A20  	        ld      hl,0x203A       ; 10    Rx bit addr

B7D3: 3A3120  	        ld      a,(0x2031)      ; 13

B7D9: 7E      	        ld	a,(hl)		;  7
B7DA: 0F      	        rrca                    ;  4

              	                        ;  here ~37 T (+/- 23 T) after start bit \_ edge
B7DE: 00      		nop			;  4

              	                        ;  here ~48 T (+/- 23 T) after start bit \_ edge

              	                        ; CHS service
